For a unit change at position p by δ (0→1 δ=+1, 1→0 δ=-1), change in P_m:
For lag t (same as m_range from -(n-1) to n-1), contribution ∑ a_{r}(1-a_{r+t}) dx removed.

Delta computation function (top-level, no closure):
```
def swap_delta(a, i, j, n, idx, a_rev, one_minus_a, out=None):
   if out is None: d = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int16)
   else: d = out; d.fill(0)
   # i zero to one
   d[idx-i:idx+n-i] += (1 - a)?? (1-a) vector changes; we can maintain one_minus = 1-a int8.
   d[i:i+n] -= a_rev
   # j one to zero
   d[idx-j:idx+n-j] -= one_minus_a
   d[j:j+n] += a_rev
   d[idx] -= 2
   d[idx + j - i] += 1
   d[idx + i - j] += 1
   return d
```
But `d` initialized each call. To avoid allocation, use a global buffer and reset via fill. But nested calls evaluation need copy? Projection formula:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2
    def f(t): return (np.clip(h+t,0,1)).sum()
    # if f at -1 maybe <target, at 1 maybe >target.
    lo,hi=-2,2
    for _ in range(80): mid=(lo+hi)/2; if f(mid)<target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    return np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```

```
def proj_grad_step(h,n,alpha,step):
   x=h.copy(); obj=smooth_obj(x)
   grad=smooth_jac(x,alpha,n)
   # maybe normalize grad; step initial step / sqrt(n)
   for _ in range(30):
       y=project_feasible(h - step*grad, n)
       f=smooth_obj(y)
       if f < obj - 1e-12? (or f <= obj): return y, f
       step *=0.5
   return h,obj
```

For objective:
```
def logsum_obj(x, alpha, n):
   G = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full')
   m = G.max()
   z = alpha*(G-m)
   z -= z.max()
   return float(m + (z.max() + np.log(np.exp(z).sum()))/alpha)
```

```
def hinge_obj(x, T, n):
   G = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
   v = np.maximum(G - T, 0)
   return 0.5*np.sum(v*v)
```

Need time management. Let's define a helper `try_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline)` top-level? It calls minimize. Since no closures. It returns best x and c.

```
def slsqp_logsum(h, n, alpha, maxiter, ftol, deadline):
   if time.time()>deadline: return h
   try:
      res = minimize(logsum_obj, h, jac=logsum_jac, args=(alpha,n), bounds=..., constraints=..., options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
      x=res.x
   except: return h
   return project_feasible(np.clip(x,0,1), n)
```

Implement binary delta function efficiently and a search loop:
```
def binary_search(a,n,deadline,max_cand=...):
  P=corr_counts(a,n)
  M=int(P.max())
  best_a=a.copy(); bestM=M
  zeros=list(np.where(a==0)[0]); ones=list(...)
  idx=n-1; a_rev=a[::-1]; one=1-a
  d=np.zeros(2*n-1,dtype=np.int16)
  temp=3.0
  for cand in range(max_cand):
    if time>deadline: break
    i = zeros[rand_int]; j=ones[rand_int]
    compute delta in d
    newM = int((P+d).max())
    if newM < M:
       accept; M=newM; if M<bestM update; update a, P, lists, a_rev, one
       temp=max(0.5, temp*0.99)
    else:
       if newM <= M and random? for later maybe accept lateral
       elif random < exp(-(newM-M)/temp): accept worse.
  return best_a,bestM
```
But if M is already integer, accepting worsens may help escape. Use temperature schedule by improvement count.

Compute delta without allocations:
```
def calc_delta(a, one, a_rev, i,j,n,idx,d):
    d[:] = 0
    d[idx-i:idx+n-i] += one
    d[i:i+n] -= a_rev
    d[idx-j:idx+n-j] -= one
    d[j:j+n] += a_rev
    d[idx] -= 2
    d[idx + j - i] += 1
    d[idx + i - j] += 1
```
But `a_rev` is a view of `a`; after updating a, `a_rev` changes. We compute delta before update, okay. `one` is separate; update. `a_rev` is a[::-1] view; when a updates, view updates. It is safe.

```
def slsqp_logsum(h,n,alpha,maxiter,ftol):
   try:
      res=minimize(logsum_obj,h,method='SLSQP',jac=logsum_jac,args=(alpha,n),... )
      x=res.x
   except: return h
   return project_feasible(np.clip(x,0,1),n)
```

```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
   a = (h > 0.5).astype(float)
   s = a.sum(); target=n//2
   if s>target: # flip ones with smallest h
      ones=np.where(a==1)[0]; diff=s-target; a[ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:diff]]]=0
   else: zeros... flip zeros with largest h
   return a
```

Alternative custom **L-BFGS with equality via augmented Lagrangian**: We can minimize smooth objective + ρ(sum - target)^2 with L-BFGS-B, projecting bounds. L-BFGS-B is faster per iteration and can handle many iterations. We can use `scipy.optimize.minimize(method='L-BFGS-B', jac, bounds, options={'maxiter':...})`. It does not enforce sum exactly, but we can add a quadratic penalty to objective and gradient. This avoids QP and can run many controlled iterations?
```
def pen_obj(x, alpha, rho, n):
    base=logsum_obj(x,alpha,n)
    r=sum(x)-n/2
    return base + 0.5*rho*r
def pen_jac(...): grad_log + rho*r*ones
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import cvxpy? Not needed.

def compute_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*dx)

def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    for _ in range(80):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       s=np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()
       if s<target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    # tiny correction for roundoff
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # adjust free middle
       mask=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
       if mask.any():
           cnt=mask.sum()
           adj=diff/cnt
           h[mask]=np.clip(h[mask]+adj,0,1)
    return h
```

`calc_delta` top-level:
```
def binary_delta(a, one, a_rev, i,j,n,idx,d):
    d.fill(0)
    d[idx-i:idx+n-i] += one
    d[i:i+n] -= a_rev
    d[idx-j:idx+n-j] -= one
    d[j:j+n] += a_rev
    d[idx] -= 2
    d[idx+j-i] += 1
    d[idx+i-j] += 1
```
But `one` is int8 (1-a). `a_rev` is int8 view. Slices can extend beyond d? Check segments:
- idx-i:idx+n-i = n-1-i : n-1-i+n = n-1-i : 2n-1-i, length n. For i=n-1: start0,end n, ok. For i=0: start n-1,end 2n-1, ok (d length 2n-1, end exclusive 2n-1).
- i:i+n length n, for i=n-1 start n-1,end2n-1, ok.
Both within.
Corrections: idx+j-i can be negative? j-i range [-(n-1),n-1], idx+... range [0,2n-2]. Good.

Penalty objective/hinge functions:
```
def pen_logsum_obj(x, alpha, rho, n):
    G=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    m=G.max()
    z=alpha*(G-m)
    z-=z.max()
    val=m + (z.max()+np.log(np.exp(z).sum()))/alpha
    r=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
    return val + 0.5*rho*r*r
```
For large alpha, `np.exp(z)` okay. If alpha very large and G difference not rounded? z max 0; okay.

Jac:
```
def pen_logsum_jac(x,alpha,rho,n):
    G=...
    m=G.max()
    z=alpha*(G-m); z-=z.max(); w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.zeros; w[argmax]=1
    w/=s
    grad=grad_from_weights(x,w,n)
    r=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
    return grad + rho*r
```
If `s` zero? Not.

`pen_hinge_obj`:
```
def pen_hinge_obj(x,T,rho,n):
   G=...
   v=np.maximum(G-T,0)
   r=float(...)
   return 0.5*float(v.dot(v)) + 0.5*rho*r*r
def pen_hinge_jac(x,T,rho,n):
   G=...; v=np.maximum(G-T,0)
   grad=grad_from_weights(x,v,n)
   r=...
   return grad + rho*r
```
For hinge, weights v not normalized; if all zero, grad zero; then L-BFGS only penalty. If T above current max, objective zero and it may drift to reduce sum? sum penalty only; after projection okay.

```
def polish_start(h,n,deadline,targ_c):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h; best_c=compute
    # L-BFGS continuation
    for alpha, rho, mi in ((10,100,200),(50,500,200),(500,1000,200),(5000,1000,250),(50000,10000,250)):
       if time.time() > deadline - 15: break
       h=lbfgs_logsum(h,n,alpha,rho,mi)
       c=compute; update
    # hinge target
    T=targ_c*0.5*n
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h=lbfgs_hinge(h,n,T,1000,300)
       update
    T2=T - 0.05
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h=lbfgs_hinge(h,n,T2,1000,250)
       update
    # SLSQP final
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       h=slsqp_logsum(h,n,20000,80,1e-12)
       update
    if time.time() < deadline - 5:
       h=slsqp_hinge(h,n,T,1000,80,1e-12)
       update
    return best_h,best_c
```

Let's craft `make_sine_start` compact:
```
def make_sine_start(n, rng):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x=np.zeros(n)
    amp=1.0
    for f in (1,2,3,5,8,13):
        x += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*f*i/n + 2.0*np.pi*rng.rand())
        amp *= 0.75
    b=0.9*np.tanh(x/(np.max(np.abs(x))+1e-12))
    h=0.5+0.5*b
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
This may have large runs? But okay.

`threshold_binary`:
```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
    a=(h>0.5).astype(np.int8)
    target=n//2
    s=int(a.sum())
    if s>target:
       ones=np.where(a==1)[0]
       order=ones[np.argsort(h[ones])[:s-target]]
       a[order]=0
    elif s<target:
       zeros=np.where(a==0)[0]
       order=zeros[np.argsort(h[zeros])[::-1][:target-s]]
       a[order]=1
    return a.astype(float)
```
If many ties, okay. For n even.

Detailed run skeleton:

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    hard=t0+budget_s-12
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # get n0,h0
    try:
       h0=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
       n0=int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
       n0=400
    if n0<50 or n0>1000 or n0%2:
       # if odd, make even 400 or adjust
       if 50 <= n0 <= 1000:
           n0=n0-1
           h0=np.asarray(h0[:n0],dtype=float)
       else:
           n0=400
           h0=None
    if h0 is None:
       h0=make_sine_start(n0,rng)
    else:
       h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
    best_h=h0.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n0)
    # if initial missing sine maybe optimize?
    # binary phase
    if n0 %2==0 and time.time() < hard-80:
       a=threshold_binary(h0,n0)
       targetM=int(np.floor(0.38075*n0/2 + 1e-9))
       dl_bin=min(hard-200, time.time()+120)  # but if budget small?
       a,bM=binary_search(a,n0,dl_bin,seed,targetM)
       cB=2.0*bM/n0
       if cB < best_c:
          best_c=cB; best_h=a.astype(float)
    # continuous phases
    # define local helper? Can't closure. We'll just call polish_start.
    # Phase polish best
    dl1=min(hard-300, time.time()+300)
    if time.time() < dl1:
       best_h, best_c = polish_start(best_h, n0, dl1, 0.38075, rng)
    # second polish from h0 if different
    if time.time() < hard-220:
       h2=h0.copy()
       # maybe noise
       dl2=min(hard-220, time.time()+180)
       h2c,b2c=polish_start(h2,n0,dl2,0.38075,rng)
       if b2c<best_c: best_c=b2c; best_h=h2c
    # noise restarts
    for sig in (0.05,0.15,0.30):
       if time.time() > hard-160: break
       h3=project_feasible(best_h + sig*rng.randn(n0), n0)
       dl3=min(hard-160, time.time()+100)
       h3c,b3c=polish_start(h3,n0,dl3,0.38075,rng)
       if b3c<best_c: ...
    # other n if not enough and time
    if best_c > 0.38075 and time.time() < hard-180:
       n1=512
       h1=project_feasible(np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n1), np.linspace(0,2,n0), best_h), n1)
       if n1%2==0 and time<hard-160:
          a1=threshold_binary(h1,n1); targetM1=int(floor(0.38075*n1/2))
          a1,bM1=binary_search(a1,n1,min(hard-160,time+80),seed+1,targetM1)
          cB1=2*bM1/n1; if cB1<best_c: update n= n1
       if time<hard-80:
          best_h,best_c=polish_start(best_h, n, hard-30, 0.38075,rng)
    # final SLSQP from best
    if time.time() < hard-15:
       hf = slsqp_logsum(best_h, n, 50000, 80, 1e-13)
       cf=compute_c5(hf,n)
       if cf<best_c: best_c=cf; best_h=hf
    if time.time() < hard-8:
       T=0.38075*n/2
       hf=slsqp_hinge(best_h,n,T,2000,80,1e-13)
       cf=compute_c5(hf,n)
       if cf<best_c: ...
```

```
def polish_start(h,n,deadline,targ_c):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    stages=((10,100,80),(100,500,80),(1000,1000,120),(10000,2000,120),(100000,5000,120))
    alpha_acc=10
    for alpha,rho,mi in stages:
       if time.time() > deadline - 8: break
       h=lbfgs_logsum(h,n,alpha,rho,mi)
       c=compute_c5(h,n)
       if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    # Hinge
    currG=np.max(np.correlate(best_h,1-best_h,'full'))
    Tunit=targ_c*0.5*n
    Tstart=currG-0.05
    if Tstart < Tunit: Tstart = Tunit
    # if currG <= target, Tstart=target maybe.
    hinge_stages=((min(Tstart,Tunit+0.2), 1000, 200), (Tunit, 2000, 200), (Tunit-0.05, 2000, 200))
    for T,rho,mi in hinge_stages:
       if T <= 0 or time.time() > deadline - 8: continue
       h=lbfgs_hinge(h,n,T,rho,mi)
       c=...
       if c<best_c: ...
    return best_h, best_c
```

`grad_from_weights`:
```
def grad_from_weights(h,w,n):
    idx=n-1
    H=1.0-h
    A=np.convolve(w,H,'full')[idx:idx+n]
    B=np.convolve(w,h[::-1],'full')[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return A-B
```
Let's test n=3 maybe: p=0 derivative formula from earlier: G_t =∑_{n_s} h[n_s+t](1-h[n_s]). For p=0:
t=-2: n_s? p-t=2 valid term h_p? Actually if p is second at n_s=p? Let's use formula: ∂G_t/∂h_p = w[(1-h_{p-t}) valid - h_{p+t} valid] for each t. For p=0,n=3:
t=-2: p-t=2 -> 1-h2; p+t=-2 invalid => w_-2(1-h2)
t=-1: p-t=1 -> 1-h1
t=0: 1-2h0
t=1: p-t=-1 invalid, p+t=1 -> -h1
t=2: p+t=2 -> -h2
So grad0 = w_-2(1-h2)+w_-1(1-h1)+w0(1-2h0)-w1h1-w2h2.
Conv A: H length3, w length5 indices 0:-2,..4:2. A[0]=conv(w,H)[idx=2] coefficient: ∑ w[s] H[2-s]? s: 0:H2 w_-2,1:H1 w_-1,2:H0 w0,H? s3? H[-1] no. So A0=w_-2H2+w_-1H1+w0H0.
B: h_rev=[h2,h1,h0]. conv(w,h_rev)[n-1:2n-1]=[indices2:4] reversed. C=conv(w,h_rev):
index2 = w0 h_rev2? h_rev2=h0? import numpy as np
```
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

EARLY_C = 0.38078
TARGET_C = 0.38075
```

Top-level constants okay.

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x, -10.0, 10.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo = -12.0; hi = 12.0
    while np.clip(x + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum() > target:
        lo *= 2.0
    while np.clip(x + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
        hi *= 2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        free = (y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            y[free] += diff / float(free.sum())
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y
```
If x has NaN? no. If free none (all at bounds) but sum diff >1e-12, can't fix; but target within capacity, should free. Good.

`compute_c5`:
```
def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * (2.0/n))
```
If n=0? no.

`make_sine_start` uses `project_feasible` defined earlier. In Python, order functions not executed until run; can call later.

`threshold_binary`: If h has sum not target, flips. If many needed, arrays.
```
def threshold_binary(h,n):
    a = (h > 0.5).astype(np.int8)
    target = n//2
    s = int(a.sum())
    if s > target:
       ones = np.where(a == 1)[0]
       order = np.argsort(h[ones])[:s-target]
       a[ones[order]] = 0  # wait ones[order] if order indexes ranks; actually order = indices in ones sorted; a[ones[order]]
```
Use:
```
       idx = np.flatnonzero(a == 1)
       flip = idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[:s-target]]
       a[flip]=0
```

Implementation of binary_search with early:
```
def binary_search(a, n, deadline, seed, stopM):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    a = np.rint(a).astype(np.int8)
    target = n // 2
    # fix sum
    s = int(a.sum())
    if s != target:
       if s < target:
          zeros = np.flatnonzero(a == 0)
          for k in range(target-s): a[zeros[k]] = 1
       else:
          ones = np.flatnonzero(a == 1)
          for k in range(s-target): a[ones[k]] = 0
    one = (1 - a).astype(np.int8)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    P = np.correlate(a.astype(np.int32), one.astype(np.int32), mode='full').astype(np.int32)
    M = int(P.max())
    bestM = M
    best = a.copy()
    if stopM is not None and M <= stopM:
       return best, M
    zeros = list(np.flatnonzero(a == 0))
    ones = list(np.flatnonzero(a == 1))
    idx = n - 1
    d = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int32)
    tmp = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int32)
    temp = 2.0
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0
    while True:
       if it % 16 == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
           break
       if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
       zi = rng.randint(len(zeros)); ji = rng.randint(len(ones))
       i = int(zeros[zi]); j = int(ones[ji])
       binary_delta(a, one, a_rev, i, j, n, idx, d)
       np.add(P, d, out=tmp)
       newM = int(tmp.max())
       accept = False
       if newM < M:
          accept = True
       elif newM == M:
          accept = (rng.rand() < 0.35)
       else:
          if newM - M <= 3:
             accept = (rng.rand() < np.exp(-(newM-M)/max(temp,0.2)))
       if accept:
          zeros[zi] = zeros[-1]; zeros.pop(); zeros.append(j)
          ones[ji] = ones[-1]; ones.pop(); ones.append(i)
          a[i] = 1; a[j] = 0; one[i] = 0; one[j] = 1
          np.add(P, d, out=P)
          M = newM
          if M < bestM:
             bestM = M; best = a.copy(); no_imp=0
             if stopM is not None and bestM <= stopM:
                return best, bestM
          temp = max(0.15, temp * 0.995)
       else:
          no_imp += 1
          if no_imp > 5000:
             no_imp = 0
             temp = max(0.15, temp * 0.6)
       it += 1
    return best, bestM
```
Potential issue: `a_rev` is a view; when `a` updated, `a_rev` updated. But `binary_delta` uses old for current candidate. We update `a` after using d. Good. `one` updated. `P` updated. `tmp` overwritten. Good.

But if `newM == M` and accept lateral, `bestM` unchanged; `M` same. Lists updated. Good.

If accept worse, M increases; best unchanged. Temperature maybe reset? We set temp maybe not. Could restart. Fine.

If M increases, `newM-M` next candidates based on current M. Good.

If no_imp large after rejects, temp lowered (or raised?) We lower from maybe 2 to1.2; but for harder, maybe should raise after long no improvement to escape? Lower makes less exploration. Actually no_imp rejects mainly worse; lowering temp may cause even more rejects. Better raise temp periodically to escape. If long_no (accept count? We reset on improvement) then raise. We'll set if no_imp >10000: temp=min(5.0, temp*1.5); no_imp=0. For lateral accepts, no_imp not reset? We reset only on improvement. Good.

`logsum_obj` and `pen_logsum_obj` duplicate G computation. Could define helper `logsum_value(G,alpha)` but top-level. Fine.

```
def logsum_value(G, alpha):
    m = G.max()
    z = alpha * (G - m)
    z -= z.max()
    return float(m + (z.max() + np.log(np.exp(z).sum())) / alpha)
```

```
def logsum_jac(h, alpha, n):

    return softmax_grad(h, G, alpha, n)
```

To reduce code, define:
```
def softmax_weights(G, alpha):
    m = G.max()
    z = alpha*(G-m)
    z -= z.max()
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = w.sum()
    if s > 0: w /= s
    return w
```
Then `logsum_obj` can use. But `logsum_obj` needs value; can compute G, m, z directly to avoid extra. Simpler.

`pen_logsum_jac` calls `grad_from_weights(h, w, n)` then `r`.

`lbfgs_logsum`:
```
def lbfgs_logsum(h, n, alpha, rho, maxiter):
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
       res = minimize(pen_logsum_obj, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=pen_logsum_jac, args=(alpha, rho, n), bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': max(100, maxiter*4), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-8})
       x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return h
    except Exception:
       return h
    return project_feasible(np.clip(x,0,1), n)
```
For L-BFGS-B, `args` passed to jac and fun. Good.

`lbfgs_hinge` similar.

`pen_hinge_obj`:
```
def pen_hinge_obj(x,T,rho,n):
    G=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')
    v=np.maximum(G-T,0.0)
    r=float(np.sum(x)-0.5*n)
    return 0.5*float(v.dot(v)) + 0.5*rho*r*r
```
`pen_hinge_jac`:
```
def pen_hinge_jac(x,T,rho,n):
    G=...
    v=np.maximum(G-T,0.0)
    if not np.any(v>0):
       # grad only penalty; but if no active, return rho*r vector? Can't know r? do.
    grad=grad_from_weights(x,v,n)
    r=...
    return grad + rho*r
```

```
def polish_start(h,n,deadline,targ_c):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    stages=((10,100,60),(100,500,80),(1000,1000,100),(10000,2000,120),(100000,5000,120))
    for alpha,rho,mi in stages:
       if time.time() > deadline - 6: break
       h=lbfgs_logsum(h,n,alpha,rho,mi)
       c=compute_c5(h,n)
       if c < best_c:
          best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    h=best_h.copy()
    Gmax=float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')))
    Tunit=0.5*n*targ_c
    T0=min(Gmax-0.02, Tunit+0.25)
    if T0 < 0: T0 = max(0.0, Tunit-0.2)
    for T,rho,mi in ((T0,1000,150),(Tunit,2000,200),(Tunit-0.05,2000,150)):
       if T <= 0 or time.time() > deadline - 5: continue
       h=lbfgs_hinge(h,n,T,rho,mi)
       c=compute_c5(h,n)
       if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

EARLY_C = 0.38078
TARGET_C = 0.38075


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    x = np.clip(x, -10.0, 10.0)
    target = 0.5 * n
    lo, hi = -12.0, 12.0
    while np.clip(x + lo, 0.0, 1.0).sum() > target:
        lo *= 2.0
    while np.clip(x + hi, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
        hi *= 2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum() < target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    y = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(y.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        free = (y > 1e-12) & (y < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if free.any():
            y[free] += diff / float(free.sum())
            y = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    return y


def make_sine_start(n, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x = np.zeros(n)
    amp = 1.0
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13):
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * i / n + 2.0 * np.pi * rng.rand())
        amp *= 0.75
    b = 0.95 * np.tanh(x / (np.max(np.abs(x)) + 1e-12))
    return project_feasible(0.5 + 0.5 * b, n)


def threshold_binary(h, n):
    a = (h > 0.5).astype(np.int8)
    target = n // 2
    s = int(a.sum())
    if s > target:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(a == 1)
        a[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[: s - target]]] = 0
    elif s < target:
        idx = np.flatnonzero(a == 0)
        a[idx[np.argsort(h[idx])[::-1][: target - s]]] = 1
    return a


def binary_delta(a, one, a_rev, i, j, n, idx, d):
    d.fill(0)
    d[idx - i: idx + n - i] += one
    d[i: i + n] -= a_rev
    d[idx - j: idx + n - j] -= one
    d[j: j + n] += a_rev
    d[idx] -= 2
    d[idx + j - i] += 1
    d[idx + i - j] += 1


def binary_search(a, n, deadline, seed, stopM):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    a = np.rint(a).astype(np.int8)
    target = n // 2
    s = int(a.sum())
    if s < target:
        z = np.flatnonzero(a == 0)
        for k in range(target - s):
            a[z[k]] = 1
    elif s > target:
        o = np.flatnonzero(a == 1)
        for k in range(s - target):
            a[o[k]] = 0

    one = (1 - a).astype(np.int8)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    P = np.correlate(a.astype(np.int32), one.astype(np.int32), mode="full").astype(np.int32)
    M = int(P.max())
    bestM, best = M, a.copy()
    if stopM is not None and M <= stopM:
        return best, M

    zeros = list(np.flatnonzero(a == 0))
    ones = list(np.flatnonzero(a == 1))
    idx = n - 1
    d = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    tmp = np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int32)
    temp = 2.0
    no_imp = 0
    it = 0

    while True:
        if it % 16 == 0 and time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if not zeros or not ones:
            break
        zi = rng.randint(len(zeros))
        ji = rng.randint(len(ones))
        i, j = int(zeros[zi]), int(ones[ji])

        binary_delta(a, one, a_rev, i, j, n, idx, d)
        np.add(P, d, out=tmp)
        newM = int(tmp.max())

        accept = False
        if newM < M:
            accept = True
        elif newM == M:
            accept = rng.rand() < 0.35
        elif newM - M <= 3:
            accept = rng.rand() < np.exp(-(newM - M) / max(temp, 0.2))

        if accept:
            zeros[zi] = zeros[-1]; zeros.pop(); zeros.append(j)
            ones[ji] = ones[-1]; ones.pop(); ones.append(i)
            a[i] = 1; a[j] = 0
            one[i] = 0; one[j] = 1
            np.add(P, d, out=P)
            M = newM
            if M < bestM:
                bestM = M
                best = a.copy()
                no_imp = 0
                temp = max(0.25, temp * 0.998)
                if stopM is not None and bestM <= stopM:
                    return best, bestM
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            if no_imp > 8000:
                no_imp = 0
                temp = min(5.0, temp * 1.35)
        it += 1

    return best, bestM


def grad_from_weights(h, w, n):
    idx = n - 1
    h1 = 1.0 - h
    A = np.convolve(w, h1, mode="full")[idx: idx + n]
    B = np.convolve(w, h[::-1], mode="full")[n - 1: 2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return A - B


def logsum_value_and_weights(G, alpha):
    m = float(G.max())
    z = alpha * (G - m)
    z -= z.max()
    val = m + (z.max() + float(np.log(np.exp(z).sum()))) / alpha
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0:
        w = w / s
    return val, w


def pen_logsum_obj(x, alpha, rho, n):
    G = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    val, _ = logsum_value_and_weights(G, alpha)
    r = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return val + 0.5 * rho * r * r


def pen_logsum_jac(x, alpha, rho, n):
    G = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    _, w = logsum_value_and_weights(G, alpha)
    g = grad_from_weights(x, w, n)
    r = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return g + rho * r


def lbfgs_logsum(h, n, alpha, rho, maxiter):
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            pen_logsum_obj, h, method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=pen_logsum_jac, args=(alpha, rho, n),
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": max(80, 4 * maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "disp": False,
            }
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return h
    except Exception:
        return h
    return project_feasible(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0), n)


def pen_hinge_obj(x, T, rho, n):
    G = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    v = np.maximum(G - T, 0.0)
    r = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return 0.5 * float(v.dot(v)) + 0.5 * rho * r * r


def pen_hinge_jac(x, T, rho, n):
    G = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full")
    v = np.maximum(G - T, 0.0)
    g = grad_from_weights(x, v, n)
    r = float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * n)
    return g + rho * r


def lbfgs_hinge(h, n, T, rho, maxiter):
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            pen_hinge_obj, h, method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=pen_hinge_jac, args=(T, rho, n),
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": max(80, 4 * maxiter),
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-8,
                "disp": False,
            }
        )
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return h
    except Exception:
        return h
    return project_feasible(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0), n)


def polish_start(h, n, deadline, targ_c):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    stages = (
        (10, 100, 50),
        (100, 500, 70),
        (1000, 1000, 90),
        (10000, 2000, 110),
        (100000, 5000, 110),
    )
    for alpha, rho, mi in stages:
        if time.time() > deadline - 6:
            break
        new_h = lbfgs_logsum(h, n, alpha, rho, mi)
        c = compute_c5(new_h, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = new_h
            h = new_h
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()

    h = best_h.copy()
    Gmax = float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")))
    Tunit = 0.5 * n * targ_c
    T0 = min(max(Gmax - 0.02, 0.0), Tunit + 0.25)
    for T, rho, mi in ((T0, 1000, 140), (Tunit, 2000, 180), (max(Tunit - 0.05, 0.0), 2000, 140)):
        if T <= 0.0 or time.time() > deadline - 5:
            continue
        new_h = lbfgs_hinge(h, n, T, rho, mi)
        c = compute_c5(new_h, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = new_h
            h = new_h
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    hard = t0 + budget - 12.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n = int(h0.size)
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None or n < 60 or n > 1000 or (n % 2):
        if h0 is not None and 60 <= n <= 1000 and n % 2:
            n -= 1
            h0 = np.asarray(h0[:n], dtype=float)
        else:
            n = 400
            h0 = make_sine_start(n, rng)

    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)

    if best_c <= EARLY_C:
        return best_h, best_c, n

    dl_bin = min(hard - 420.0, t0 + 0.10 * budget)
    if n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < dl_bin:
        a = threshold_binary(h0, n)
        stopM = int(np.floor(TARGET_C * 0.5 * n + 1e-9))
        a, m = binary_search(a, n, dl_bin, seed, stopM)
        cB = float(2 * m) / n
        if cB < best_c:
            best_c = cB
            best_h = a.astype(float)
        if best_c <= EARLY_C:
            best_h = best_h.astype(float, copy=True)
            return best_h, compute_c5(best_h, n), n

    dl1 = min(hard - 300.0, t0 + 0.43 * budget)
    if time.time() < dl1:
        hh, cc = polish_start(best_h, n, dl1, TARGET_C)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    dl2 = min(hard - 220.0, t0 + 0.64 * budget)
    if time.time() < dl2:
        h2 = project_feasible(h0 + 0.02 * rng.randn(n), n)
        hh, cc = polish_start(h2, n, dl2, TARGET_C)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    dl_rt = min(hard - 180.0, t0 + 0.84 * budget)
    for sig in (0.04, 0.10, 0.22):
        if time.time() > dl_rt or best_c <= EARLY_C:
            break
        h3 = project_feasible(best_h + sig * rng.randn(n), n)
        hh, cc = polish_start(h3, n, min(dl_rt, time.time() + 90.0), TARGET_C)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hh

    if best_c > EARLY_C and n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < hard - 180.0:
        n1 = 512
        h1 = project_feasible(
            np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n1), np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n), best_h),
            n1
        )
        if time.time() < hard - 130.0:
            a1 = threshold_binary(h1, n1)
            stopM1 = int(np.floor(TARGET_C * 0.5 * n1 + 1e-9))
            a1, m1 = binary_search(a1, n1, min(hard - 130.0, time.time() + 70.0), seed + 1, stopM1)
            c1 = float(2 * m1) / n1
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_c = c1
                best_h = a1.astype(float)
                n = n1
            if best_c <= EARLY_C:
                return best_h, compute_c5(best_h, n), n

        if time.time() < hard - 45.0:
            hh, cc = polish_start(best_h, n, hard - 25.0, TARGET_C)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hh

    if best_c > 0.38070 and time.time() < hard - 10.0:
        hf = project_feasible(best_h + 1e-6 * rng.randn(n), n)
        hf = lbfgs_logsum(hf, n, 20000, 5000, 90)
        cf = compute_c5(hf, n)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_c = cf
            best_h = hf
        T = max(TARGET_C * 0.5 * n - 0.02, 0.0)
        hf = lbfgs_hinge(hf, n, T, 5000, 110)
        cf = compute_c5(hf, n)
        if cf < best_c:
            best_c = cf
            best_h = hf

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c, n
```
